#UC2 port: every change to the engine, where and why

Review report for the owner, 2026-10-08. The engine (engine-uc2) is reviewed as of main 4813dd9. The third-person jitter fix merged after that (c59c02d) has its own section, 50. The frontend (ufrontend) is reviewed as of branch uc2 5654da2.

Four writers produced sections 10-40 by reading the code and the notebooks. The coordinator wrote this overview, section 50 and the generated appendices. Nothing was re-run for this report: measurements are quoted from LOG.md and the notebooks, labelled measured, seen only or not verified.

#How to read this

  • Start with "Start here" below. It lists what most needs your eyes and the decisions waiting on you.
  • Sections 10-50 cover one area each. Every feature has the same blocks:
    • what you see
    • where (file:line and commits)
    • how the parts fit (native vs script vs ini vs frontend, and what you get if one part is missing)
    • why
    • decisions and alternatives
    • disadvantages, risks, deviations
    • status
    • off switch
  • Each section ends with two lists: "Open issues in this area" and "Places where we changed Epic's shared code".
  • The appendices are generated from git, not written by hand. Use them to check the sections.
    • Appendix A: every one of Epic's files we modified, with the commits that touched it.
    • Appendix B: our new modules.
    • Appendix C: every ini key that differs from Epic's.
  • Line numbers refer to the snapshot 4813dd9. git -C engine-uc2 show 4813dd9:<path> shows the exact file.
Section Area Features
10 Platform, build, runtime: layout, CMake, Core, porting fixes, package loading, the web runtime, -ini:, Windows, 64-bit, inis, tools inventory 14 + inventory; all 136 modified Epic files classified
20 Renderer: the GL driver, shader translation, post-processing, projectors/shadows, gamma, AA, GL4ES/WebGL R-1 .. R-22
30 Input, UI, scripts, profiles and saves: SDL input, pads, the mouse (D-6), UI box, profiles, names, text entry, console, AutoFOV, SaveConfig, rebinding, the script compiler, rebuilt packages 30.1 .. 30.18
40 Audio, movies, networking and the frontend 40.1 .. 40.20
50 Third-person character jitter (O-11), merged after the snapshot 1
A-C Generated: modified Epic files with commits; new modules; ini keys vs Epic's

#Start here: what most needs your review

  1. Three script packages are no longer Epic's.
    • The asset tree ships rebuilt GUI.u, UCGameplay.u and UCMenu.u (since D-4, D-6 and D-9; Phase D allowed script changes).
    • The mouse look is the one feature that stops working with the shipped UCGameplay.u; the other two packages carry the player name without a profile and the KEYBOARD / MOUSE page (table at the top of section 30).
    • They keep the shipped package GUIDs. Peers with the shipped and the rebuilt UCGameplay.u (a server package) pass the GUID check but run different code. Never mix them.
    • Rebuilding from source also changes CTF: Epic's load-time CTFFlag.home patch is defective. On the shipped package a returning flag never runs its Begin: code. Ours does, so an enemy standing on the base gets the flag after 0.1 s instead of on the next touch. Not tested in a game (30.18).
  2. We modified 136 of Epic's files, about +7,100/-2,900 lines. Section 10 classifies all of them:
    • 95 are porting fixes only (compile, portability, 64-bit, bugs found while porting);
    • 18 mix a porting fix with a feature;
    • 23 are feature changes only (renderer gates, input, UI, saves, network fixes, movies).
    • Every Xbox-only path we needed is lifted through a named render capability. _XBOX is never defined.
  3. The mouse (section 30.5) is the model of how layers combine:
    • SDL2Drv delivers raw relative counts (C++).
    • UCPlayerInput saves them before Epic's stick code overwrites the look axes, then adds them back linearly (script, UCGameplay.u).
    • DefUser.ini binds the mouse to aTurn/aLookUp, and UC2InputMigrate.cpp updates old User.ini files. (Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 00 and 30.5.)
    • The section has a table of your complaints against the measured cause, before and after.
  4. The renderer aims at the Xbox look, mostly without an Xbox picture to compare (section 20).
    • Glow/bloom strength, dynamic gamma, distortion size, the Xbox shadow filter and the projectors are "seen only" or checked against Epic's code. A few xemu shots would settle the biggest ones.
    • Owner-tuned values sit one setting away from the Xbox's: DOF alpha 65 (O-3), distortion fade 15000 instead of 5000 (O-7), blurs sized for 480 lines (O-10), AA 4x / anisotropy 16x (O-8).
  5. The web build is WebGL 1 only, through GL4ES. A device without WEBGL_depth_texture loses depth of field and distortion occlusion (no fallback yet). (Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 00 and R-20.)
  6. Audio follows Xbox XACT behaviour on OpenAL Soft, with zone reverb. Nobody has compared it with a real Xbox (mix level, HRTF colour, reverb loudness) (section 40). Movies are Theora/Vorbis, and their picture in a real browser has never been checked by a test.
  7. Networking:
    • The join and packet-loss fixes are in Engine/Core (TcpNetDriver itself is unchanged), and both ends need them.
    • WebRTC works for one host and one guest.
    • A guest closing the browser tab pauses the host's game for up to 60 s. That is UC2's standby check, left as Epic wrote it (40.12-40.15).
  8. Some frontend changes reach every target (section 40.17-40.18). Package versions 145-151 in the shared glue's readers, and the shared name-ownership and user-files code. The older targets' output was recorded as byte-identical after the change; that check was not re-run here.

#Found while writing this report (not fixed: nothing was changed for the report)

  • Renderer
    • DOFShippedBlurAlpha is True in the inis but its compiled default is 0 (UC2GLRenderDevice.cpp:214). An ini without the key gets the non-shipped alpha.
    • The device turns dynamic gamma back on at every session start, so a player can only switch it off per session.
    • The GL4ES device reads DistortionCullDistance only from the [UC2GLDrv.UC2GLRenderDevice] section; the copy in its own section is ignored.
    • Stale text: BLOCKERS.md still says the projector shaders and PS_Terrain3Layer are not drawn; a comment in UnRenDev.h names a file without a gate; a comment on UseXboxProjectors overstates. LOG.md has no O-3 entry.
  • Platform
    • README.md and BUILD.md still say "Status: M0", and BUILD.md has no web section. UC2_ASSETS_DIR is documented but read by nothing.
    • The web meta.json does not record the fetched libraries.
    • BLOCKERS.md contradicts itself on arm64.
    • Default.ini has been LF since the M7 merge (a plain diff with Epic's CRLF file is noisy; use Appendix C).
    • engine-ut2004's "fail-soft loader" was not ported (real exception catching is used instead).
    • Whether the exit-time dedicated-server crash/hang (M13, BLOCKERS.md) is gone is not recorded.
  • Audio / network / frontend
    • audio.md says category volumes below 0.1 are silent; the code plays them at -10 dB.
    • WebRTCDrv.h and webrtc.md say the data channel is unordered; the one the browser tests measured is reliable and ordered.
    • The test console commands (NETPLACE, ...) and -NETOLDACKS are compiled into the shipping engine.
    • Frontend branding/help.md, portal-notes.json ("must not be published as playable"), the target README and STATE.md still describe the first-light build.
    • The web manifest is 1,991 MB against a 2,048 MB cache budget; sound, music and movies are not precached.

Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 00 ("Found while writing").

#Never verified (by any test or person)

  • Real hardware and software:
    • real Windows (wine only);
    • a physical game pad (SDL's virtual pad only; pads are off on the web);
    • AMD or Intel GPUs (Mesa llvmpipe and an NVIDIA Quadro P620 only);
    • Safari, Firefox, phones;
    • an arm64 configure or build.
  • Comparisons with the Xbox: most of the renderer's look (item 4 above) and the audio mix and reverb.
  • Feel: the mouse and pad were measured by script; no person has judged them.
  • Network: team games and CTF online, package download, more than three clients, two web guests, travel over WebRTC, in-game server discovery on the web.
  • Never exercised in a game: split-screen, voice stealing, outro cinematics, a movie material with sound.

#What is built, per platform

  • Built (root CMakeLists.txt UC2_PACKAGES):
    • Epic's, ported: Core, Engine, IpDrv, UWeb, UnrealGame, UCElements, UCGameplay, GUI.
    • Ours:
      • UC2ALAudio (OpenAL Soft);
      • UC2Movie (Ogg Theora/Vorbis);
      • SDL2Drv (window, input, pads);
      • UC2GLDrv (OpenGL renderer);
      • UC2GL4ESDrv (the same renderer bound to GL4ES: WebGL and the native GLES twin);
      • WebRTCDrv (web only);
      • SDLLaunch (the game executable).
    • Editor + UCC (ucc-bin, the commandlet host and script compiler; the Editor only for 32-bit Linux).
  • Not built, kept in the tree as reference: D3DDrv, WinDrv, Window, Launch, XboxDrv, XboxAudio, XboxLive, XboxLaunch, UIX, UnrealEd, UnrealUtil, Bundler, DebuggerLaunch, FNameDebug, GUIDesigner, XboxAssetConversion, SDLDrv (UT2003-era SDL 1.2).
  • Script-only packages: their shipped .u are used, except GUI, UCGameplay and UCMenu, which are rebuilt.
  • Targets:
    • native Linux 32-bit (the reference) and 64-bit;
    • Windows i686/x86_64 (MinGW, run under wine only);
    • web (Emscripten, wasm32);
    • arm64 (preset only).

  • Open: O-6 (needs your spot), O-14 (needs your example), E-1, E-3, E-4 (stopped), E-5 (needs your go-ahead), M15 content polish, M16 original Xbox assets, M17 close-out. (Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 00 (milestones).)

#The rules every change followed

  • Epic's code is changed as little as possible, and never by defining _XBOX, XBOX_ASSET_FORMAT or LIN_LOAD. Where the PC needed an Xbox-only path (post-processing, projectors, shadows), the gate is lifted through a named capability of the render device (FRenderCaps), and the PC branch is kept.
  • Removing an effect is never a fix. The community's "fixed maps" are reference only.
  • Behaviour that is tuning or mapping is ini data. The Xbox value is the default unless you chose otherwise.
  • Every visible feature has a repeatable test in tools/dev/. The Xbox oracle, xemu, is a last resort.

#10. Platform, build and runtime

Scope: everything that makes the engine compile, link, load and run on Linux, Windows (MinGW) and the Web, except the renderer, input/UI/scripts, audio/movies and networking (other sections). Baseline 7633696 (import of Epic's tree); code as of main 4813dd9, read from the snapshot /home/ion/WorkUC2/engine-uc2-report. Line numbers are from that snapshot. The frontend (ufrontend) was not read for this section: where it matters the text says what the notebooks and LOG.md record about it.

#The things to know

  1. One tree, three platforms, the same code paths. Epic's Win32/Xbox tree is built with GCC 15 (-m32 by default), Emscripten 5.0.7 (wasm32) and MinGW-w64 13 (i686 by default). 32-bit Linux is the reference: its pointer width and struct layout equal wasm32 and i686 MinGW, and the shipped script packages (.u, version 151, licensee 0) were compiled for 32-bit pointers. Every package is a static library linked whole into the executable (__STATIC_LINK, as the Xbox XBE did). _XBOX, XBOX_ASSET_FORMAT, LIN_LOAD, LIN_SAVE and USE_LINEAR_LOADING are never defined; Xbox-only paths we need are lifted behind named capabilities.
  2. Core's platform layer is not Epic's MSVC one. Core/Inc/UnGnuG.h, Core/Src/UnUnix.cpp and Core/Inc/FFileManagerUnix.h come from engine-ut2004 (the sibling UT2004 port) with UC2's platform contract added back; threading is new pthread code (UnThreadingPosix.*). Epic's UnVcWin32.* and UnThreadingWindows.* stay in the tree, unbuilt. MinGW uses the same GCC layer with _WIN32 branches, not the MSVC layer.
  3. We edited 136 of Epic's files (git diff --name-status 7633696 4813dd9 -- Source, status M), and added 187 new ones (127 of them the GL driver). 95 of the 136 are porting fixes only (compile, portability, 64-bit, bugs found while porting), 18 mix a porting fix with another section's change, 23 belong to other sections only. The full table, with a reason per file, is at the end of this section.
  4. Struct layout is the hazard, and one tool guards it. Script and C++ must agree on every native class layout. Guards: #pragma pack(4) on hand-written mirror headers and GCC_PACK(4) tags in generated ones; at 64-bit a table of 50 "placeholders" for script ints that hide C++ pointers; ucc-bin verifyclasses (32-bit: 713 size and offset checks; 64-bit: 400 classes, 6,578 scripts scanned); and UGameEngine::LoadMap still runs Epic's own check, fatal on mismatch. tools/dev/layout-diff.py and dwarf-layouts.py compare whole-engine layouts across compilers.
  5. The web build is a threaded wasm module. The engine runs on a worker (PROXY_TO_PTHREAD), yields to the browser once per frame through Asyncify, catches C++ exceptions through JavaScript (not wasm) exceptions, reads game files through a WasmFS JS-implemented backend the frontend's glue provides, and has an explicit 2 GB memory ceiling. The artifact names are fixed: SDLLaunch.js, .wasm, .data, meta.json.
  6. What has and has not been run. Linux x86 32-bit and x86_64: fully (maps, client, GL, audio, movies, controls, network). Windows: only under wine 10 (never on real Windows). arm64: a preset and a toolchain file exist; no notebook records a configure or a build. Web: engine side by Node and by the frontend's own browser specs (boot, audio, saves, net, keys), run by the agents and re-run by the coordinator; I did not re-run anything.
  7. Every run goes through bin/capped (memory ceiling and time limit in one systemd scope), after the incident of 2026-10-04 when an unbounded headless-browser test took the owner's desktop down. CLAUDE.md makes this a rule.

#What is built and what stays unbuilt

UC2_PACKAGES in CMakeLists.txt:408-412; a package joins the build only if Source/<Pkg>/CMakeLists.txt exists (CMakeLists.txt:438-445).

Package Role Linux Windows Web
Core, Engine, IpDrv, UWeb, UnrealGame, UCElements, UCGameplay, GUI Epic's native packages, all code kept yes yes yes
UC2ALAudio, UC2Movie our audio device (OpenAL Soft) and movie player yes yes yes
SDL2Drv, SDLLaunch our SDL2 client/viewport and the launcher (uc2.bin, uc2.exe, SDLLaunch.js) yes yes yes (Emscripten's SDL2 port)
UCC commandlet host (ucc-bin, ucc.exe, ucc-bin.js for Node) yes yes Node only
Editor Epic's script compiler (ucc-bin make), header exporter 32-bit Linux only no no
UC2GLDrv our OpenGL render device yes yes yes
UC2GL4ESDrv its GL4ES binding (GLES 2 / WebGL 1) yes (the "twin") no yes
WebRTCDrv browser network driver no no yes

Editor is removed for 64-bit and MinGW at CMakeLists.txt:433-435 (its hand-written UEditorEngine mirrors script's int Pad[8] with four pointers, correct only at 32 bits). UCC and Editor are in UC2_NATIVE_ONLY_PACKAGES (CMakeLists.txt:417); the web build removes UCC, SDL2Drv and SDLLaunch from that list again (:425-427).

Kept in Source/ but never compiled (reference or dead): D3DDrv (the specification of the GL driver), XboxDrv (pad mapping), XboxAudio (XACT behaviour), XboxLive, XboxLaunch, WinDrv, Window, Launch (the Win32 launcher), UnrealEd, UIX, SDLDrv (SDL 1.2), Setup, Bundler, XboxAssetConversion, GUIDesigner, DebuggerLaunch, FNameDebug*, UccDepend, BuildProcess, ShaderData (read by tools/psh2glsl.py, not compiled), UnrealUtil. In Core: UnVcWin32.cpp, UnThreadingWindows.cpp, FNameDebug.cpp, FContiguousMalloc*.cpp, UnPSX2.cpp, UnGCN.cpp. In Engine: UnSVehicle.cpp and palette.cpp are in no Visual Studio project and are removed from the glob (Source/Engine/CMakeLists.txt). The script-only packages (Gameplay, UCInventory, UCEffects, UCCharacters, UCMutators, UCAnnouncements, UCMenu) are compiled by ucc-bin make (M14), not by CMake. Verified: git diff --stat 7633696 4813dd9 shows no change in any of the unbuilt packages.

#Features

#1. Repository layout and the import of Epic's tree (M0)

What the player/owner sees - nothing at run time. The owner sees a repo where git diff 7633696 4813dd9 is the complete, auditable list of changes to Epic's code.

Where

  • Import: commit 7633696 import: Epic's Avaris source tree, text files only, LF line endings, produced once by tools/dev/import-epic-sources.py (it refuses to overwrite; it is the record, not a sync tool). Rules and totals: docs/SOURCE-LAYOUT.md.
  • Imported: 3,448 text files with CRLF changed to LF, 82 Windows-native project files byte for byte, 570 binaries skipped (of 16,451 files in the pristine tree; reference/epic-sources.sha256). Folders: Source/<42 packages>, Thirdparty/{nvTriStrip,nvDXT,DirectX8} (sources/headers only), Engine/Config/{Default,DefUser,DefaultXbox, DefUserXbox,KeyBinds}.ini (byte for byte, the baseline our config diffs against), docs/epic/ (five build notes).
  • Replaced at import: Thirdparty/zlib is engine-ut2004's 1.1.4 (Epic's was 1.1.3; Thirdparty/zlib/zlib.h:40), which has a CMakeLists. Copied from engine-ut2004 byte for byte: vendor/glue/*, cmake/TargetArch.cmake, cmake/toolchain-mingw-w64.cmake.
  • Scaffold: 547bd73 build: CMake scaffold, presets, docs and the porting plan.
  • Policy files: .gitattributes (* -text: git must not touch line endings or encoding), CLAUDE.md (agent rules, workspace table, memory rules), AGENTS.md (points to CLAUDE.md), BUILD.md, README.md.
  • Not in the repo, on purpose: assets (<WorkUC2>/assets/uc2, built by tools/dev/mkassets-uc2.sh), build-*/, anything from reference/, Epic's Visual Studio import binaries (.lib, icons, textures).

How the parts fit - the pristine tree lives outside the repo (<WorkUC2>/epic-sources, never edited); this repo holds the text part; content never enters it. A change to Epic's code is therefore always visible against 7633696.

Why - the layout is the repo standard of engine-ut2004 (Source/<Package>/{Inc,Src}, Thirdparty/, Engine/Config/, vendor/glue/, cmake/), so the frontend and the sibling ports treat all engines alike.

Decisions and alternatives

  • LF once at import, Latin-1 kept (sources and some .uc files contain 0x02 bytes). Alternative rejected: re-encode to UTF-8 (would change the bytes the compiler and package scripts see).
  • Keep unbuilt Epic code under Source/ instead of deleting it: D3DDrv, XboxAudio, XboxDrv are the specification of the new drivers; deleting anything needs the owner (CLAUDE.md).
  • Epic's SDL 1.2 driver (Source/SDLDrv) and launcher were stale UT2003 leftovers; SDLLaunch was rewritten for SDL2 (original in git history), SDLDrv stays as dead reference.

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • Against epic-sources a diff needs --ignore-cr-at-eol (the pristine tree is CRLF).
  • Latin-1 files: grep here is ugrep and silently skips them; the rule is grep -a, find is bfs. A tool that re-encodes a file corrupts it.
  • README.md and the top of BUILD.md are stale: README says "Status: M0 (scaffold). Nothing compiles yet beyond zlib"; BUILD.md says "Status: M0". BUILD.md has no section for the web build or for the artifact.
  • Engine/Config/Default.ini is no longer a CRLF file (see feature 13): "byte copy of Epic's" is not true any more.
  • The unbuilt packages are about 650 files (ShaderData 336, UnrealEd 113, Bundler 30, XboxDrv 28, and smaller ones) that nobody compiles; they are kept as reference and are not in the CMake build.

Status - M0 gate: pristine tree checked by sha256sum -c after all M0 work, 0 mismatches (LOG.md, 2026-10-04); git ls-files holds no package, media or binary file (LOG.md, same). Measured now: 4,431 tracked files (Source 3,575, Thirdparty 703, tools 86).

Off switch - none.

#2. Build system: CMake, presets, flags

What the player/owner sees - cmake --preset native && cmake --build build-native -j12 makes uc2.bin and ucc-bin; source env.sh && cmake --preset wasm-perf && cmake --build build-wasm-perf makes the web artifact; the windows* presets cross-build uc2.exe/ucc.exe.

Where

  • CMakeLists.txt (445 lines), commits 547bd73 (scaffold), 9b303b8 UCC: ucc-bin links (M2); 4-byte struct packing on every target, e030ac7 build: fetch SDL2 2.32, be2044b build: Ogg/Vorbis vendored, OpenAL Soft fetched, 44932d1 build: ucc-bin as WebAssembly for Node, 754ce47/1e7f700 (MinGW), 34d2e39 (Theora, UC2Movie), b85f15f (GL4ES), 2a0d111 (arm64, native64-uchar), 58cf932 (sanitizer preset, -Werror=int-to-pointer-cast), 19ca6e1 (OpenAL Soft on the web), 1538155 (PipeWire headers kept out of MinGW), 93686c0/24c80dc (Editor). Sections:
    • options UC2_ALLOW_64BIT, UC2_WEB_PERF, UC2_WEB_DEBUG, UC2_ASSETS_DIR (:30-34), UC2_SANITIZE (:132), UC2_UNSIGNED_CHAR_AUDIT (:137);
    • architecture and pointer width: cmake/TargetArch.cmake, then UC2_PTR_BYTES (:44-69) because CMAKE_SIZEOF_VOID_P does not see the -m32 demotion; the demotion itself (:71-85) adds -m32 to compile, link and probe flags and CMAKE_FIND_LIBRARY_CUSTOM_LIB_SUFFIX;
    • global definitions (:91-123): PLATFORM_32BIT, LINUX=1 __LINUX_X86__ (all non-Windows targets, wasm included), __STATIC_LINK=1 (:38), NO_UNICODE_OS_SUPPORT=1, __FILE_NOCASE=1, SUPPORTS_PRAGMA_PACK=1, MinGW -static -static-libgcc -static-libstdc++ (:99-105);
    • compiler flags (:127-156): -fsigned-char -fno-strict-aliasing -pipe; GCC adds -fpermissive -fno-lifetime-dse -Wno-register, and on 64-bit -Werror=int-to-pointer-cast; clang adds -fdelayed-template-parsing;
    • Emscripten flags (:158-178): one -O per configuration (-O3 -g0 perf, -O0 -g debug, -O2 -g default), -pthread everywhere, and include(UC2Web);
    • dependency blocks: zlib (:184-185), SDL2 (:193-224), Ogg/Vorbis (:232-233), OpenAL Soft native/Windows (:237-288) and web (:300-349), Theora (:353), GL4ES (:361-400);
    • package list and native-only list (:408-445).
  • CMakePresets.json (109 lines): configure presets native, native64, native64-uchar, native64-asan, arm64, wasm, wasm-debug, wasm-perf, windows, windows64; matching build presets.
  • cmake/TargetArch.cmake (engine-ut2004's), cmake/toolchain-mingw-w64.cmake (engine-ut2004's: picks <arch>-w64-mingw32-gcc-posix when present, finds libraries only in the cross sysroot), cmake/toolchain-aarch64-linux-gnu.cmake (new, 26 lines, M13), cmake/UC2Web.cmake (new, 44 lines, M4w; feature 8).
  • One CMakeLists.txt per package in Source/ (generated pattern: all Src/*.cpp, GPackage=G<pkg>Package, ThisPackage=<pkg>, link to Engine/Core). Source/Core/CMakeLists.txt lists files explicitly; Source/UCC/CMakeLists.txt and Source/SDLLaunch/CMakeLists.txt hold the per-platform link blocks.
  • Tests: the gate of each milestone is a clean configure and build of the presets; tools/dev/m13-cast-census.sh counts pointer-width diagnostics in a build log.

Presets:

Preset Output What
native build-native/ Linux x86 32-bit (-m32); default and reference
native64 build-native64/ Linux x86_64 (LP64)
native64-uchar build-native64-uchar/ x86_64 with -funsigned-char (audit build: arm's char)
native64-asan build-native64-asan/ x86_64 with ASan + UBSan (no vptr/alignment)
arm64 build-arm64/ aarch64 cross, build-only; never configured
wasm build-wasm/ web, -O2 -g, assertions on (dev)
wasm-debug build-wasm-debug/ web, -O0
wasm-perf build-wasm-perf/ web, -O3 -g0, no assertions: what ships
windows build-windows/ MinGW-w64 i686, posix threads, cross
windows64 build-windows64/ MinGW-w64 x86_64 (LLP64)

How the parts fit - the root file decides architecture, definitions and flags, then fetches third-party code with the engine's definitions saved and restored around it (OpenAL Soft and GL4ES must not see -fpermissive or -Ds; the OpenAL block also forces CMAKE_SIZEOF_VOID_P=4 for its own configure under the -m32 demotion). Each package is a static library; UCC and SDLLaunch link all of them with --whole-archive so class registrants and native tables survive, plus -Wl,-export-dynamic on Linux because Core finds a linked-in package by dlsym("GLoaded<Pkg>") (MinGW and Emscripten do not need it). A new package needs its own CMakeLists and a name in UC2_PACKAGES; without the launcher link-list entry its classes are not registered.

Why

  • Epic's Visual Studio solution and the stale UT2003 makefiles cannot build here; engine-ut2004 solved the same problem, so its flags and arrangement were taken (gnu++14, -fpermissive, -fno-strict-aliasing, __STATIC_LINK, --whole-archive).
  • -fno-lifetime-dse: Epic calls destructors explicitly and reuses the object (UArrayProperty::DestroyValue runs ~FArray() then LoadConfig adds to the same array); the compiler deletes the destructor's stores as dead and the next AddZeroed writes into freed memory. Found with ASan on the wasm build (docs/porting/analysis/web-smoke.md defect 3). GCC spelling at CMakeLists.txt:145, clang spelling in cmake/UC2Web.cmake:34.
  • SUPPORTS_PRAGMA_PACK=1 for every target: Epic's projects compile with /Zp4 and generated headers wrap themselves in #pragma pack(push,4); Core.h decided from __LINUX__, which is not defined yet at that point under GCC, so packing was silently off (M2 finding, 9b303b8).
  • -Og for Core/Src/UnCorSc.cpp and UnProp.cpp on GCC (Source/Core/CMakeLists.txt): "FFrame::Step reference juggling miscompiles at -O2" (found in the UE1/UT2004 ports; same VM code).
  • Z_PREFIX (CMakeLists.txt:184) prefixes zlib symbols.

Decisions and alternatives

  • 32-bit as default instead of x86_64: matches wasm32 and i686 MinGW, and the script layout. 64-bit is UC2_ALLOW_64BIT.
  • Static libraries linked whole instead of DLLs per package (what the PC build did): the Xbox XBE did the same; no GetProcAddress tables, no --export-all-symbols. Cost: no per-package DLL modding.
  • Emscripten flags set per configuration with -O blanked in CMAKE_*_FLAGS_* so nothing appends a second -O.
  • Makefile generator ("Unix Makefiles") in every preset, as engine-ut2004.

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • -fpermissive hides a class of errors: narrowing pointer-to-int casts compile as warnings (M13 had to build a census script for them, tools/dev/m13-cast-census.sh); two-phase-lookup and Sort<INT>/Compare forward-declaration problems pass the same way. New code of ours is compiled with it too.
  • Fetched libraries use floating refs: SDL2 release-2.32.x, OpenAL Soft ufront/web, GL4ES fix/cubemap-bind-active-unit (branches/tags, GIT_SHALLOW), no commit pin; the first cmake --preset needs network; a moved branch changes the build. emit-artifact-meta.mjs records repo@branch for this reason, but Web.cmake does not pass --dep-* arguments, so the web meta.json lists only the Emscripten version (gl4es and OpenAL Soft, which the web build does fetch, are not recorded; the comment in the emitter, "no fetched libraries on the web yet", is stale).
  • UC2_ASSETS_DIR (CMakeLists.txt:33, documented in BUILD.md) is defined and read by nothing.
  • -Og on the two VM files costs script speed on GCC builds (native, MinGW); not measured. Clang (wasm) has no such rule.
  • The wasm preset's description says "with DWARF"; the dev link uses -g2 (names only) on purpose (Source/SDLLaunch/cmake/Web.cmake:12-22: DWARF made a 45 MiB module that did not link in 3 GB).
  • emcc/clang crashed with a signal at -j10/-j12 (UnModel.cpp, UnDemoPenLev.cpp, UnRenderBSP.cpp); re-running the same build continues. Not a source problem (notebooks), but not understood either.
  • ASan, UBSan and arm64 builds are opt-in; none runs by default.

Status - M0 gate (presets configure), M1 (libCore.a, 0 errors on both compilers), M2 (8 libraries and ucc-bin link, 0 undefined, 0 multiply defined; ucc-bin 45 MB), measured, LOG.md 2026-10-04. Each later merge logs "native/windows/wasm-perf build" green (A4-A13 entries). native64 first link: 6 min 10 s, 0 errors (64bit.md step 1). arm64: not verified (never configured, BLOCKERS.md).

Off switch - the options above; -DUC2_SANITIZE=, -DUC2_ALLOW_64BIT=OFF.

#3. Third-party libraries, fetched libraries, vendor/glue

What the player/owner sees - sound and movies need these; uc2.exe ships with OpenAL32.dll beside it; nothing else to install on Linux except an OpenAL runtime that is built with the game.

Where

  • Vendored in Thirdparty/ (never under Source/):
    • zlib 1.1.4 (13 sources, CMakeLists.txt): used by Core/Inc/FFileManagerGeneric.h (compressed-file reads), so Core links it PUBLIC; Core's .cpp files do not use it.
    • Vorbis/ogg, Vorbis/vorbis: libogg 1.0 / libvorbis "20011231" (1.0 release) as vendored by engine-ut2004, copied byte for byte (CRLF kept), only the build-relevant parts; README.uc2; commit be2044b. UC2ALAudio decodes the cooked Ogg Vorbis from memory with vorbisfile.
    • Theora: libtheora 1.2.0 decoder only (official tarball, sha-256 recorded in README.uc2), no assembly, plain C on every target; libogg 1.0 lacks ogg_uint16_t/ogg_uint64_t, so the CMakeLists defines both as macros for it and for everything that links it; used by UC2Movie (M9).
    • nvTriStrip, nvDXT, DirectX8: Epic's headers/sources, imported for reference. No CMakeLists names any of them (only Engine.vcproj mentions nvTriStrip); the Engine code that needs nvDXT is guarded _MSC_VER (feature 5).
  • Fetched by CMake into build-*/_deps/ (never vendored): SDL2 release-2.32.x (static, X11 only with Wayland off and X11 linked, not dlopened; SDL2main only for MinGW; on the 32-bit build /usr/lib/i386-linux-gnu is added to the search paths or SDL's X11 probes link 32-bit objects against 64-bit libs and fail silently); OpenAL Soft from 10-p/openal-soft-web branch ufront/web (native: SHARED libopenal.so.1, loaded with dlopen when the audio device starts, wave-file and null backends on, EAX off; Windows: built as OpenAL32.dll, WASAPI/DirectSound/WinMM, Linux backends off since the host's PipeWire headers broke MinGW (1538155); web: STATIC with the Web Audio backend, built with its own flags); GL4ES from 10-p/gl4es branch fix/cubemap-bind-active-unit (static, GLES 2 back end, no X11/EGL of its own, no init constructor; Linux and web only; its -O2 -w overrides the engine's flags).
  • On the web SDL2 is not fetched: cmake/UC2Web.cmake:39-44 defines an INTERFACE target SDL2::SDL2-static carrying -sUSE_SDL=2, so SDL2Drv/SDLLaunch CMakeLists are identical on both toolchains.
  • vendor/glue/: ue_abi.h (ABI version 1), wasmfs-shim.js, webrtc-shim.js, proxynet-shim.js, ue_proxynet_abi.h. All byte copies of engine-ut2004's; one commit (547bd73), unchanged since. Linked: wasmfs-shim.js (feature 8) and webrtc-shim.js (E-2). proxynet-shim.js and ue_proxynet_abi.h are not linked: the owner decided "WebRTCDrv ported; ProxyNetDrv not" (PLAN.md).

How the parts fit - Web.cmake reads UE_ABI_VERSION from ue_abi.h and passes it to the meta emitter; the frontend checks the artifact's ABI against its own glue. wasmfs-shim.js forwards WasmFS hook calls to Module.UE.fs.backend, which the host's glue injects before main(); on the worker thread it builds a Proxy table lazily and importScripts the glue bundle ufront-glue.js (so the engine thread can read asset bytes synchronously) and also delivers ufrontExec messages to _ue_exec_command on the engine thread. Without the injected Module.UE the shim throws [ue-abi] ... not injected or ABI mismatch.

Why - Ogg/Vorbis/Theora and OpenAL Soft are what the cooked audio and movies need (M8, M9); SDL2 replaces SDL 1.2; GL4ES is the engine-ut2004 route to GLES 2/WebGL 1; the glue is the frozen engine-to-frontend contract.

Decisions and alternatives

  • Fetched, not vendored, for SDL2/GL4ES/OpenAL Soft (rule in CLAUDE.md), so the forks keep their history.
  • OpenAL Soft built from source for Windows instead of engine-ut2004's prebuilt soft_oal.dll: the brief forbids downloading binaries.
  • dlopen of OpenAL (never DT_NEEDED): a machine without it still boots, silent, and nothing of OpenAL runs before the engine allocator exists.
  • Theora decoder only, bundled assembly left out: fewer files, same output on every target.

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • Floating refs (see feature 2). The OpenAL block saves and restores CMake state around the fetch (directory definitions, C++ standard 20, CMAKE_SIZEOF_VOID_P); a CMake upgrade could break it quietly.
  • The vendored glue files must stay identical across four engine repos; webrtc-shim.js must not be edited here.
  • libvorbis 1.0 ignores the end granule: decoded waves are cut to their source length in UC2ALAudio (3ab01aa).
  • PipeWire (BLOCKERS.md): the 32-bit game falls back to PulseAudio unless libpipewire-0.3-modules:i386 is installed; a player needs the same package for native PipeWire.

Status - fetch-from-empty-directory builds were done at M4 (SDL2) and noted in the M4 gate; Windows OpenAL32.dll imports checked (objdump -p, package-windows.sh); web OpenAL/GL4ES: browser specs (LOG.md 2026-10-06/07). Not verified: a build with the fetch refs pointing at different commits.

Off switch - none for the libraries; audio can be selected off by -nosound, movies by -nostartupmovies/-nomovie.

#4. Core's platform layer (M1)

What the player/owner sees - nothing directly. This is what gives the game a clock, files, threads, error handling and a command line on Linux, Windows (MinGW) and the web. A wrong clock showed up as "Negative delta time!" in a quarter of server runs before it was fixed (below).

Where (all under Source/Core/; commits 82c9fd3 Core: shared headers and sources compile with GCC 15 and em++, 9faf167 Core: portable platform layer (UnUnix.cpp rebased on engine-ut2004, pthread threading), e7fc07d Core: Unix file manager from engine-ut2004, portable word wrap, header fixes for launchers, c36e3d2 notebook; later fixes named below)

  • Epic's code changed:
    • Inc/UnGnuG.h (477 lines; the baseline was a stale UT2003 Unix header): rebased on engine-ut2004's UT2004 header, then given back UC2's contract, which is UnVcWin32.h's PC branch. Gate or fix: platform selection for __LINUX_X86__ (every non-Windows target, wasm included) and __MINGW32__ (:22-106); UT_NO_X86_INTRIN on every non-x86 target (:110); GCC_PACK(n) is aligned(n) only (:163, UT2004's choice; the old UC2 header had packed,aligned(n)); HANDLE/HINSTANCE are void* (:152); TCHAR_TO_ANSI, ANSI_TO_TCHAR, TCHAR_TO_OEM are casts that drop const, as on PC, and defining UNICODE without NO_UNICODE_OS_SUPPORT is an #error (:314-320); GET_VARARGS* through va_list (:202-224; MSVC reads the stack); appTrunc, appSqrt(FLOAT), appInvSqrt, LARGEINT (:351-372); appCycles()/appCycles64() use rdtsc on x86 and a microsecond counter otherwise (:381-405); PTRINT (:270-276); characters must be signed unless the audit build is on (:284); _rotl/_rotr C fallbacks (:430-460). Not taken from UT2004's header: its math-intrinsics block, GBaseCycles, GIsMMX, GIsAltivec, the wide-char layer, BUGGYINLINE, UREG_*.
    • Src/UnUnix.cpp (3,923 lines): engine-ut2004's file (UTF-8 punctuation in comments made ASCII), with: USystem replaced by UC2's (:1115 on; Exec commands CONFIGHASH, EXIT/QUIT, DEBUG CRASH/GPF/RECURSE/EATMEM, DIR, NAMEHASH, VIEWNAMES, DUMPNAMES; RELAUNCH is UT2004's Unix version); DoCPUID on <cpuid.h>; EdLoadErrorf with UC2's body (:3586); and the UC2 platform contract at :3603-3923: appGetSystemErrorMessage, appDebugMessagef (log + stderr, no message box), appGetLastError, appSystemTime64 (local-time Win32 FILETIME, :3654), appSystemCmd, appContiguousMalloc/Free (plain GMalloc, check(dwAlign==4) as on PC, :3703), appMemCPUFastAccess (no-op), appSqrt(FLOAT), appLogva/FTraceLog under DEBUG_LOGGING, appGetLanguageID (always LC_INT, :3772), ExternalContigMalloc/Free, and FFileStream::Read/Create/Destroy on stdio plus the file-streaming pthread (:3803-3920, started from appPlatformInit at :3221; if the thread cannot start FFileStream::Destroyed=2 so appExit does not wait forever). Kept from UT2004 although UC2 does not declare them: clipboard helpers, appGetTime, MinGW/Android/MacOSX branches (eases later 3-way merges).
    • Inc/FFileManagerUnix.h (932 lines, new): engine-ut2004's Unix file manager (FFileManagerLinux.h is now a 3-line shim: typedef FFileManagerUnix FFileManagerLinux; so the launchers keep their include and class name). UC2 changes: no load statistics; GetUserDir() a plain member (UT2004's virtual would change UC2's vtable); Windows FindFirstFile wildcard semantics in WildcardMatch (:831) and SearchDirectory (:864); Exists() also true for directories (:604); UT_MKDIR without a mode on _WIN32 (:24-28); O_BINARY on every open (MinGW opens in text mode otherwise, :32-35); USERPROFILE for the user directory on Windows; -usehomedir opt-in (:727-760, default no home directory).
    • Inc/FFileManagerGeneric.h: vendored zlib by path; POSIX stat in CompareFileTimes; DeleteFiles cuts the pattern at the last \ or / (commit 10b802f: with only \ nothing was deleted on POSIX and deleting a profile never worked).
    • Inc/UnFile.h: check/checkSlow were __noop, an MSVC intrinsic; now ((void)0) off MSVC (:155-176). NEW T[n] placement form operator new[](size_t,const ANSICHAR*,INT); sized operator delete(void*,size_t) and delete[] (C++14 sized deallocation would otherwise hand GMalloc memory to free); UT_REPLACEMENT_ALLOC (always_inline, :735-743) so the replacement global new/delete are never emitted out of line (otherwise the linker routes the whole program, including Emscripten's own start-up allocations, through GMalloc).
    • Inc/UnMath.h: x87 ASMTransformPoint/Vector only ASMLINUX && !UT_NO_X86_INTRIN, and TransformPointBy/VectorBy take the C path on GCC (#elif 0 //ASMLINUX, as UT2004's shipped Linux code: "apparently broken --ryan").
    • Inc/UnTemplate.h, UnMem.h, UnName.h, UnType.h, UnObjBas.h, UnUnix.h, Core.h, UnAnsi.cpp, UnGUID.cpp, UnName.cpp, UnObj.cpp, wordwrap.*: compile fixes (feature 5).
    • Src/UnMisc.cpp:2033 appInit: no longer drops the first command-line token off MSVC (:2052; the portable launchers pass arguments only, so it ate the map URL, commit 63bb34e); appItoa/appI64toa implemented off MSVC with snprintf (:359, :382; they were appErrorf("Not implemented") and the first client run died in SpawnPlayActor).
    • Src/UnUnix.cpp:1962 appSecondsSlow: clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC) minus a once-initialised origin (commit 1321d55). engine-ut2004 accumulated gettimeofday differences in two statics; UC2's file-streaming thread and the game thread raced on them and the clock stepped backwards.
    • Src/UnUnix.cpp:2075 appSleep: on Emscripten emscripten_sleep only when emscripten_has_asyncify(), else usleep (commit 5bd7f07); :3015 no CPUSPEED= needed where there is no cycle counter (clock is microseconds, GSecondsPerCycle pinned to 1e-6). :2835-2843 writes into the 32-character GMachineCPU are bounded (commit a6c8edc; UT2004's code overflowed it, found by ASan).
  • New code of ours: Inc/UnThreadingPosix.h (199 lines), Src/UnThreadingPosix.cpp (786 lines): pthread versions of every class in UnThreadingBase.h (FCriticalSectionPosix recursive mutex, FEventPosix with manual/auto reset and Pulse, FMutexPosix, FSemaphorePosix, FSynchronizeFactoryPosix, FQueuedThreadPosix, FQueuedThreadPoolPosix, FRunnableThreadPosix, FThreadFactoryPosix, and the GSynchronizeFactory/GThreadFactory globals that UnThreadingWindows.cpp defines on Win32). Inc/UnMovieBase.h (movies, other section) holds FMovieThread/FMoviePlayer moved out of UnVcWin32.h.
  • Launcher duty: SDLLaunch.cpp:88-89,502-503 and UCC.cpp:112-117,531-532 install FSynchronizeFactoryPosix/FThreadFactoryPosix before appInit (it creates critical sections); -rdynamic and whole-archive linking (feature 2).
  • script / ini / frontend: none.
  • Tests: nm -C --defined-only build-native/Source/Core/libCore.a shows exactly one strong definition of each listed UC2 symbol (core-port.md Step 5); the tools/dev/map-census.sh census and every later run use this layer.

How the parts fit - the compiler picks UnGnuG.h (Core.h by __GNUG__); Core.h includes UnUnix.h for __UNIX__ || __MINGW32__; the implementation is UnUnix.cpp for every non-MSVC target; the file manager the launcher instantiates is FFileManagerUnix; threading is the Posix classes. Epic's UnVcWin32.* was an MSVC-only layer with inline __asm, #pragmas and SEH; its UC2-specific functions (the Scion additions Epic's UT2003-era Unix layer lacks) were re-created one by one in UnUnix.cpp from the PC branch, replacing each Win32 call with its POSIX equivalent.

Why - UC2's own UnGnuG.h/UnUnix.cpp were UT2003-era leftovers with no appContiguousMalloc, appSystemTime64, appGetLanguageID and the rest, so Engine did not compile (1,193 errors in 107 files at the baseline, engine-baseline.md; the biggest classes were appTrunc/appTan undeclared 225 each, __noop 161, packed-field binding 212). engine-ut2004's layer was already GCC/Emscripten/MinGW-proven for the same UE2 code base.

Decisions and alternatives

  • Rebase on engine-ut2004's UnUnix.cpp rather than port Epic's UnVcWin32.cpp: the latter is MSVC-only; the former existed and ran.
  • Threading as a new pthread file implementing Epic's abstract UnThreadingBase.h interfaces, rather than rewriting the callers (UnMisc.cpp, UnMaterial.cpp, the movie thread): small, and Win32 semantics (recursive critical section, manual/auto-reset events, PulseEvent) are reproduced exactly on top of mutex and condition variable. Differences are by necessity: no TerminateThread (a thread that does not stop is detached and its handle leaked), no SuspendThread (logs a warning), priorities and affinity are recorded only, a dying queued thread does not call ReturnToPool.
  • GCC_PACK stays aligned(n) and layout is fixed with #pragma pack instead (feature 6).
  • Contiguous (GPU) allocator: plain GMalloc, as the PC build; FContiguousMalloc*.cpp stays unbuilt.
  • TCHAR is ANSI on every target (Epic removed Unicode in UC2: "jg -- Removed unicode support", Core.h); never -fshort-wchar and never UNICODE.
  • x86 asm to C (the replacements, all in Epic's files): FloatToInt becomes lrintf on every target that is not 32-bit x86 (Engine/Src/UnRenderStaticMesh.cpp:18, UnRenderBatch.cpp:1197); the two F32vec4 SSE blocks in Engine/Src/UnRenderUtil.cpp:45,186 are #if __HAS_SSE__ (an MSVC-only switch) so GCC runs the scalar loop that was the else; the x87 transforms in UnMath.h (above); rdtsc/cpuid only on x86 (UT_NO_X86_INTRIN otherwise). The inline asm bodies of ASMTransformPoint/Vector remain in UnMath.h for x86 builds but nothing calls them.

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • Bugs in Epic's code that were left (core-port.md "Suspected real bugs"): USystem::Exec DIR has a fixed 512-entry buffer; appSystemCmd tests Code<=32 (ShellExecute's convention) on a system() result so success (0) reports "CmdFailed"; TMultiMap::operator=/Add name TMapBase<TK,TI> instead of the 3-argument form; TScopePointer frees with delete what NEW TCHAR[] allocated; UnThreadingBase.h FRunnable/FRunnableThread lack virtual destructors; FQueuedThreadWin::Run calls ReturnToPool while the pool holds the thread-queue lock (the Posix version skips it); FFileStream::Destroyed is a plain INT polled across threads; UObject::IsValid/GetFullName test this against NULL (UnObj.cpp:235) and GCC/clang may delete such checks at -O2: -fno-delete-null-pointer-checks is not set. None has failed in a run.
  • Not adopted from UT2004: its 32-byte allocation padding on 64-bit in FMallocAnsi (no padding exists in Core/Inc/FMallocAnsi.h); UC2 relies on the native-size guard instead (feature 12).
  • The file-streaming thread polls every 1 ms forever (usleep(1000)) and nothing in UC2 creates a stream outside Core yet. Decision for the web: kept (pool slot).
  • appBaseDir is the working directory plus / (UnUnix.cpp), not the executable's directory: the game must be started with System/ as working directory on every platform.
  • UnUnix.cpp carries Mac, PSX2 and GCN branches nobody builds.

Status - measured: M1 gate cmake --build ... --target Core 0 errors on GCC 15 -m32 and emcc (LOG.md 2026-10-04); symbols check; M3 census 58/58 uses it. The clock race: "Negative delta time!" gone after the fix (runtime-port.md item 8; the rate before, "about a quarter of runs", is the agent's count). Not verified: the Posix threading classes beyond what the engine uses (queued thread pool, semaphores): used by UnMisc.cpp and UnMaterial.cpp only.

Off switch - none. -usehomedir opts in to a home directory.

#5. Making Engine and the game natives compile (M2): the porting-fix catalogue

What the player/owner sees - nothing; this is the groundwork. ucc-bin links with 0 undefined and 0 multiply defined symbols.

Where (the per-file table is at the end of this section; commits f700305 Engine: compiles with GCC 15 (-m32) and emcc 5.0.7, 482cfbb IpDrv, UWeb, UnrealGame, UCElements, UCGameplay, GUI: build natively and for the web, 9b303b8 UCC: ucc-bin links (M2); 4-byte struct packing on every target). The change classes, each with its sites:

  1. __noop (MSVC) as a compiled-out macro: Core/Inc/UnFile.h (check, checkSlow), Engine/Inc/Engine.h (CheckNotBool), Engine/Inc/UnRenDev.h (BEGIN_D3D_EVENT(x) becomes { UC2_D3D_EVENT_NOOP plus a variadic no-op, because it is used as BEGIN_D3D_EVENT(n)(colour,fmt,...)).
  2. Implicit int, extra qualification on in-class declarations, two-phase lookup, template<>: UnTemplate.h (FArray::Realloc, TScopePointer::operator=/&, this-> in TTransArray/TLazyArray/TMultiMap, TPooledList nested parameters renamed), UnName.h, UnType.h, UnMem.h (defaults moved from the friend operator new declarations to the definitions), APawn.h, UnPlayerStats.h, UnChan.h, UnRenderResource.h, Engine/Src/UnRender.cpp, UnParticleEmitter.cpp, UnRenderVisibility.cpp (explicit PoolInstance specialisations need template<>), UnCamMgr.cpp (Compare(UViewport*&,UViewport*&)), FContentManager.cpp (pointer-to-member syntax), Editor/Src/UnVisi.cpp, IpDrv/Src/MasterServerUplink.cpp, Editor/Inc/Perlin.h (register INT), UnPrim.h (namespace-scope CompareHits).
  3. Invalid token pasting: Engine/Src/UnStats.cpp (10 macros: ## before ., (, [, =; MSVC ignores it).
  4. Inline functions defined in one .cpp and called from others: MSVC emits a callable copy, GCC/clang do not (13 unresolved at the first link). The inline/FORCEINLINE marker was removed (declaration and definition) from AActor::GetGravity, IsJoinedTo, AddToRecentMap; UFont::GetCharSize; UReachSpec::supports; ULevel::IsPaused; UParticleEmitter::UpdateRelativeRot; FDynamicActor::UpdateSorting (Engine/Inc/UnRender.h); ALevelInfo::NotifyPauseChange; UGUIComponent::SaveCanvasState/RestoreCanvasState (in the generated GUIClasses.h and its source GUI/Classes/GUIComponent.uc, so a regeneration keeps the change); free functions FLinterp (UnUCRibbonEmitter.cpp, UnUCTrailEmitter.cpp), CheckCanvasClip (GUI/Src/UnGUICore.cpp).
  5. FColor converts to both FPlane and FVector: FPlane(FColor) is ambiguous in ISO C++. Three sites in Engine and one in UCElements call .operator FPlane() (the conversion that keeps alpha): UnRenderStaticMesh.cpp:541, UnRenderBatch.cpp:1057,1060, UCElements/Src/UCElements.cpp:143.
  6. An FString object passed through ...: UnTex.cpp (two debugf), UnObj.cpp SUPPRESS/UNSUPPRESS, UnUnix.cpp DIR. Harmless on MSVC by layout accident, undefined elsewhere.
  7. A typeless constant: UnParticleEmitter.cpp:267 static const INT MIN_ATTEN_DIFF = 0.2f; - Epic's static const MIN_ATTEN_DIFF = 0.2f; was an int, so 0 and the "skip if almost equal" test never skips. Kept as shipped (typed INT, value still 0).
  8. Windows-only code: UnModelLight.cpp nvDXT block && _MSC_VER; xbstrip.cpp (standalone XDK sample; basic typedefs instead of <D3D8.h>, Win32 headers on MinGW, pre-standard for-scope variables, const operator<); GUI/Src/GUI.cpp (<wtypes.h> only on Windows); GUI/Inc/UnUIXInteraction.h (ULONGLONG, HRESULT, BOOL, S_OK off Windows); UnInteraction.cpp (literal function name where guard() omits __FUNC_NAME__ on __LINUX__); Core/Src/UnAnsi.cpp (copysign, atoll, const results of strstr/strchr/strrchr); IpDrv sockets (feature 11).
  9. Packing: Core.h honours SUPPORTS_PRAGMA_PACK from the build (it decided from __LINUX__, not yet defined at that point); hand-written mirror headers wrapped in #pragma pack(push,4) (feature 6, 12).
  • UCC (Source/UCC/Src/UCC.cpp, 426 lines added): the __STATIC_LINK block did what XboxLaunch/Src/Launch.cpp does for the static XBE after appInit - clear GNativeLookupFuncs, add the two Core lookups, AUTO_INITIALIZE_REGISTRANTS_* for every package and RegisterNames{GUI,UCElements,UCGameplay}; UWeb's hand-written static header registers classes but not native lookups, so FindUWebUWebResponseNative/FindUWebUWebRequestNative are added (:540-565).

How the parts fit - Epic's files keep their MSVC branches; every change is either an #if or a form both compilers accept. The _XBOX-only branches are untouched except where a named capability lifts a gate (renderer/movie sections).

Why - the 1,193 baseline errors (engine-baseline.md) were 7 classes of problem; the gate was zero errors without stubbing and without leaving out any file of the PC project (only UnSVehicle.cpp and palette.cpp, which are in no project).

Decisions and alternatives

  • FPlane(FColor) chosen as the alpha-keeping conversion on the assumption that MSVC 7.1 did the same. Unverified assumption: if lighting alpha looks wrong, UnRenderStaticMesh.cpp:541 and UnRenderBatch.cpp:1057,1060 are the places.
  • Keep a shipped bug (MIN_ATTEN_DIFF = 0) rather than change behaviour.
  • -fpermissive stays (feature 2) rather than a clean conformance pass: the Epic tree is about a million lines.
  • No SSE path outside MSVC: a portable SSE/NEON convex-volume check is "a possible later optimisation, not a correctness issue" (engine-port.md).

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • Removing inline from 13 functions changes performance characteristics: they are no longer inlined across translation units (not measured).
  • FloatToInt via lrintf rounds to nearest in the current mode; x87 frndint+fistp did the same; no known difference.
  • A transient emcc crash at -j12 (re-run fixes it).
  • "invalid use of incomplete type" in UnObjBas.h/UnClass.h and 1,128 -Wwrite-strings warnings remain (LOG.md M1).

Status - measured: M2 gate, 0 errors on both compilers for 8 libraries; ucc-bin links; run with no run tree it stops at Epic's missing-ini error (as expected). Later regressions: every merge rebuilds native, windows and wasm-perf.

Off switch - none.

#6. Loading v151 packages headless (M3): the layout guard, the bugs found by running, the census

What the player/owner sees - ucc-bin server DM-Deadbolt brings the level up and ticks; 58 of 58 maps do the same; ucc-bin verifyclasses prints all native class layouts match.

Where (commits 44f81c6 Runtime: objects construct correctly and native class layouts match the shipped .u, 63bb34e, 1321d55 Core: monotonic, race-free appSecondsSlow; map census tool; M3 notes, dcf245f)

  • Layout guard:
    • Epic's own check: Engine/Src/UnGame.cpp:2046-2062 includes the generated EngineClasses.h (and the lists at Engine/Inc/EngineClasses.h:10802-11012, #ifdef VERIFY_CLASS_SIZES) at every LoadMap; appErrorf("C++/ Script class size mismatch") on a difference. This ran only once a map had loaded - too late to diagnose a failure that stops loading.
    • ucc-bin verifyclasses (UCC/Src/UCC.cpp:287-331 VerifyNativeClasses, dispatch :594): loads Engine, IpDrv, UnrealGame, UCElements, UCGameplay, GUI (64-bit also UWeb; with the Editor build, Editor) and runs the same generated lists. LOG.md: 713 checks over six packages, started at about 120 mismatching classes.
    • tools/dev/layout-diff.py (8a232bd): parses every TU of the wasm build for wasm32 and for i386-pc-linux-gnu with -fdump-record-layouts-complete and prints every record that differs; tools/dev/dwarf-layouts.py does the same from DWARF of two builds; tools/dev/fix-bitfield-pack.py (44f81c6) applies the GCC tagging rules to class headers.
  • What was wrong (in order of discovery, each fixed in Epic's file):
    1. Core/Inc/UnObjBas.h:457 DECLARE_CLASS built objects with new(...) TClass(): value-initialisation zero-fills a class without a user constructor, wiping Class/Name/Outer/Index. Now new(...) TClass (UT2004 has this).
    2. UWeb natives not found (UCC.cpp:550-551, above).
    3. Core/Src/UnObj.cpp:3776 UObject::ResolveName used strcpy on overlapping buffers; glibc's optimised copy corrupted class names (UCDamageType -> UCDaocgeType). Now appMemmove.
    4. C++ and script disagreed on class layout (crash in AActor::PostLoad: Brush held the float 1.0). Three causes: the header exporter tagged a bool GCC_PACK(4) when its CPF_Transient differed from the previous bool, which MSVC ignores and UBoolProperty::Link packs together (17 tags removed; AActor was 580 instead of 576); the exporter did not tag the first member of a class, so GCC put a small first member into the parent's tail padding (Itanium ABI), which MSVC and the script linker never do (183 first members tagged); and hand-written class headers needed the same rules (UnTerrain.h, UnCameraEffects.h, UCCameraEffects.h). The exporter Editor/Src/UnEdExp.cpp is fixed too so a regeneration agrees. Counted today in the generated headers: EngineClasses.h has 131 lines with a GCC_PACK tag added and 8 removed (git diff); GUIClasses.h, IpDrvClasses.h, UCElementsClasses.h, UCGameplayClasses.h, EditorClasses.h carry the rest.
    5. UCGameplay/Inc/UCCameraEffects.h had fallen behind UCScreenDOF.uc: FirstPersonAlpha and ThirdPersonAlpha were missing, so AbsoluteBlurAlpha sat at FirstPersonAlpha's offset; MSVC's size check still passed. Members added. A deliberate deviation from the shipped binary - see "Disadvantages".
    6. appInit ate the first command-line token (feature 4); the map argument was lost.
    7. Port 1000 (the Xbox system-link port) is privileged on Linux: Engine/Config/Default.ini [URL] Port=7777.
    8. The clock race (feature 4).
    9. (M4) FFileManagerUnix::SearchDirectory used strstr, so Localization\int\*.INT (Xbox language codes are upper case) matched no *.int file: the registry (UObject::CacheDrivers) was empty and ucc server short names, ucc help and game-type lists found nothing. Fixed by the real wildcard matcher (feature 4); Localize() itself was never affected.
  • ini derivation: Core/Src/UnMisc.cpp:2118-2146 (Epic's code): Avaris.ini is created from Default.ini, and User.ini from DefUser.ini, when absent; the module name is Avaris (GModule, set in SDLLaunch.cpp:493; ucc-bin also), which also names the log (Avaris.log) and the product strings (Localization/int/avaris.int).
  • Path and case: appUnixPath (UnUnix.cpp:3474) turns \ into / and, with __FILE_NOCASE (always set), resolves case per path component through find_alternate_filecase (:3432) only when access() fails; FFileManagerUnix calls it for every open, stat, mkdir, rmdir, chdir.
  • The census: tools/dev/map-census.sh <build-dir> [secs] [workers] (1321d55, 555078d): each worker has its own run tree and port; a map passes when Bringing Level ... up for play is logged, no Critical: line, and it is still ticking when timeout stops it. CENSUS_NAME/CENSUS_PORT keep a second census apart.
  • Config: Engine/Config/README.md.

How the parts fit - the generated *Classes.h headers carry both the C++ declarations and the size/offset lists; the script linker (UStruct::Link) lays out the shipped .u properties; the check compares the two. At 32 bits every layout rule is "the compiler's, plus the tags"; at 64 bits the link-time guard adds the placeholder table (feature 12).

Why - the shipped .u files cannot change before M14 (rebuilding them from source), so C++ must adapt. Each item above was found by running, not by reading (runtime-port.md).

Decisions and alternatives

  • Fix layout by tags and pragmas, not by editing script: script is the fixed side.
  • The gate "ucc-bin batchexport of 17 .u" in PLAN.md was replaced by the engine's own layout lists because batchexport lives in Editor (not built then): "a more direct test of the same thing" (LOG.md).
  • Default.ini Port 7777 (UE2 default) rather than keep 1000.

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • UCScreenDOF alpha is a deliberate behaviour difference from the shipped Xbox binary: script sets AbsoluteBlurAlpha to ThirdPersonAlpha (100) or FirstPersonAlpha (65) per camera; with Epic's stale header native code always read 65. We read what the script intends. The owner later decided "depth-of-field alpha = the shipped value 65" (DOFShippedBlurAlpha=True in Default.ini, BLOCKERS.md O-3); that switch lives in the renderer section.
  • verifyclasses does not check script structs on 32 bits ("does NOT check structs", web-smoke.md); struct and event-parameter layout is covered by layout-diff.py/dwarf-layouts.py and, at 64 bits, m13-layout-check.py. A C++-only record that is bulk Serialized against a file format would not be caught; "none found by inspection of the list", "not exhaustively searched".
  • ComboMatinees/*.unr (9 maps) are not on the package search path and are not in the census.
  • Census PASS is weak on exit: timeout without -k reports 124 whatever happens after the TERM. An exit-time crash of the dedicated server (BLOCKERS.md "Open (M13)": UObject::StaticExit -> PurgeGarbage -> ULinkerLoad::DetachExport, about 2 maps in 58, both widths) is listed as open there; the network branch fixed related exit-time use-after-frees (006e725), but no later note records whether the census crash is gone: not established.
  • Missing Class Editor.TransBuffer is logged for 56 of 58 maps (Editor not built in the web and 64-bit configs; the throw is caught). With the Editor package linked (32-bit Linux) the class exists.

Status - measured: verifyclasses 713 checks, 0 mismatches (M3 gate); ucc-bin Engine.ServerCommandlet CTF-Octane, 60 s, Game class is 'CTFGame', clean shutdown; census maps: 58 PASS: 58 FAIL: 0, twice; repeated at 64-bit (58/58, same per-map result as 32-bit).

Off switch - none. ucc-bin verifyclasses is a command, not a setting.

#7. Client boot and the launch flow (M4)

What the player/owner sees - uc2.bin (Linux), uc2.exe (Windows) or the web page starts, creates a window, brings Entry.MyLevel and then Menu.MyLevel up, opens UCMenu.UC2MainMenu, and ticks. (Input mapping, pads and UI are other sections.)

Where (commits e030ac7 build: fetch SDL2 2.32 (static, X11), 49189ba SDL2Drv: SDL2 client and viewport..., 3d7e719 SDLLaunch: uc2.bin, the SDL2 game launcher; portable client in Default.ini, later 34dd131, 8db663c, 34d2e39, 8ff02cd, 9df0ad4, 1efa52e, 941326a)

  • Source/SDLLaunch/Src/SDLLaunch.cpp (645 lines; Epic's SDL 1.2 launcher rewritten, original in 7633696) and SDLLaunchPrivate.h. Flow in main (:471):
    1. setbuf unbuffered stdout/stderr, appArgv0(argv[0]), command line = argv[1..] joined with single spaces (no re-quoting, max 1,024 characters).
    2. strcpy(GModule,"Avaris"), GPackage = appPackage() (:493).
    3. GIsClient=GIsGuarded=1; Posix factories; appInit(...) with FMallocAnsi, FOutputDeviceFile, FOutputDeviceAnsiError, FFeedbackContextAnsi, FFileManagerUnix, FConfigCacheIni::Factory (:504).
    4. Static registration (:513-565): native lookups, AUTO_INITIALIZE_REGISTRANTS_* of every linked package (Engine, SDL2Drv, UC2GLDrv, UC2GL4ESDrv, UC2ALAudio, UC2Movie, WebRTCDrv, IpDrv, UWeb, UnrealGame, UCElements, UCGameplay, GUI), the UWeb lookups, RegisterNames*.
    5. Mode flags: GIsServer=1, GIsClient = !-SERVER, GIsEditor=0, GIsScriptable=1, GLazyLoad = !GIsClient || -LAZY. -LOG echoes the log to the terminal. -BENCHMARK -SECONDS=n runs 30 Hz steps and logs the average.
    6. SelectRenderDevice() (:309): -NULLRENDERER, -OPENGL (UC2GLRenderDevice), -GL4ES (UC2GL4ESRenderDevice) write [Engine.Engine] RenderDevice for the run if the device is in the build. OverrideResolution() (:442): -640x480 ... -1600x1200 written to the active client's FullscreenViewportX/Y.
    7. InitEngine() (:371): ReplaceMissingDrivers() (:333) replaces a ViewportManager, RenderDevice or AudioDevice the ini names but this build does not contain (with SDL2Drv.SDL2Client, Engine.NullRenderDevice, Engine.NullAudioDevice) and logs it - this is why an ini from the PC or Xbox release still boots; in the web build also switches NetworkDevice=IpDrv.TcpNetDriver to WebRTCDrv.WebRTCNetDriver (a browser has no sockets); -testrendev=; Running.ini semaphore file (deleted at clean exit); FirstRun; UGameEngine is constructed from ini:Engine.Engine.GameEngine, UEngine::GEngine = Engine before Engine->Init() (UC2-specific, the PC and Xbox launchers do it), Startup time is logged.
    8. After init: EXEC= file, LOADWINDOWPOSITION, the start-up logos (appPlayMovieFullscreen("..\\Movies\\UC2_Logos.xmv", FSMOVIE_Startup, 1), skippable, :604), MainLoop (CMainLoop::RunLoop, :170), GRenDev->Flush, delete Running.ini, appPreExit, appExit.
    • CMainLoop::RunLoop (:170): tick (Engine->Tick(DeltaTime); fixed 1/30 s while benchmarking or recording), tick-rate limiter by appSleep (:219-241; -maxfps=<n> caps a standalone game, added for D-6 tests), on the web the Asyncify yield (:242-247), then Viewports(0)->UpdateInput(0,0), which pumps the SDL events.
    • Left out of the PC WinMain on purpose: config wizard, log window, installer mutex, the D3D hardware probe (SetInitialConfiguration), the exec hook with property windows. consolecommand= logs that it is not available.
  • Source/SDL2Drv (input section) is the client and viewport the launcher selects. Engine/Config/Default.ini: ViewportManager, RenderDevice, AudioDevice, [SDL2Drv.SDL2Client] (copy of [WinDrv.WindowsClient]); USDL2Client::StaticConstructor gives UClient the same values as compiled defaults, because with Epic's own Default.ini (no such section) the render device got 0x0.
  • Tests: the M4 gate (below); tools/dev/m10-controls.sh runs uc2.bin under Xvfb for every later check.

How the parts fit - SDLLaunch.cpp is the only place that knows the list of linked packages; adding a package means a registrant call there and in UCC.cpp, a CMake link-list entry and a UC2_WITH_* definition. The null devices are always available, so any ini boots.

Why - the launcher structure is engine-ut2004's; what UC2 does at start-up comes from the PC WinMain (Source/Launch/Src/Launch.cpp, Engine/Inc/UnEngineWin.h) and the static registration from XboxLaunch/Src/Launch.cpp.

Decisions and alternatives

  • Module name Avaris so ini, log and product strings are the PC game's.
  • One SDL window per viewport, as WinDrv created one Windows window per viewport. Split screen on PC is an owner question (BLOCKERS.md, unresolved).
  • -nullrenderer selection is in the engine-ut2004 style (switch table), not an ini edit by the user.

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • Command line is limited to 1,023 characters (silently truncated at an argument boundary).
  • ucc help prints blank names/descriptions for native commandlets (HelpCmd/HelpOneLiner are localized properties and are empty on the class default object UCC.cpp reads). Whether the same blankness affects script reading class'X'.default.<localized> on a native class was "worth checking as soon as text is on screen"; the menus do show text, from which I infer it does not matter for the menu path, but no note closes the question (BLOCKERS.md).
  • Default.ini suppresses the DevUI/DevGUI log names, so menu activity is not in the log by default; tests write their own Avaris.ini without those two lines.
  • Ctrl+letter control characters, IME, the UnrealScript debugger, consolecommand= are not provided (client-boot.md).

Status - measured, M4 gate (LOG.md 2026-10-04): uc2.bin -log under Xvfb, still running at the 40 s timeout, Opened viewport (SDL window 1, 640x480), Render device: Engine.NullRenderDevice, 0 Critical, 1 Warning (Editor.TransBuffer); ucc-bin server DM-Deadbolt by short name; ucc-bin help; real X key events reached UCMenu.UC2Arena_Main.

Off switch - -nullrenderer, -nosound, -nostartupmovies, -nomovie; ReplaceMissingDrivers cannot be switched off.

#8. The web runtime (M4w, M11 runtime parts)

What the player/owner sees - the game runs in a browser tab: the frontend loads SDLLaunch.js/.wasm/.data, streams game files on demand, and the engine boots to the menu and plays. The owner's preview runs on port 8096 (published with bin/publish-preview).

Where (commits 44932d1 build: ucc-bin as WebAssembly for Node (exceptions caught, -fno-lifetime-dse, NODERAWFS, pthreads), 5bd7f07 Core: appSleep blocks where there is no Asyncify; no CPUSPEED= needed..., 8a232bd Engine: 4-byte packing for the hand-written script mirrors UnTex.h and UnParticleSystem.h, 45f7301 SDLLaunch: browser build (...), per-frame Asyncify yield, ue_exec_command, 51a55f0 build: explicit 2 GB wasm memory maximum, 429c17b build: the dev web build links at -O2 with names only, 604a8a3 Core, Engine: a leading Key=Value token is a global option, not the map, 81aea5f merge, 34b4ed2 GL4ES link flags, 19ca6e1 OpenAL Soft on the web / pool 4, a860ac8 SDLLaunch: web ue_exec_command passes what the engine does not take to the first viewport, 941326a WebRTC)

  • new code of ours:

    • cmake/UC2Web.cmake (44 lines), included from CMakeLists.txt:174-178: -sNO_DISABLE_EXCEPTION_CATCHING at compile time for every target and UC2_WEB_EXCEPTION_FLAGS for every link (:24-25); -fno-lifetime-dse for clang (:34); the SDL2::SDL2-static INTERFACE target carrying -sUSE_SDL=2 (:39-44). (Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 10, feature 8.)

    • Source/SDLLaunch/cmake/Web.cmake (116 lines), included from Source/SDLLaunch/CMakeLists.txt:61-64; output SDLLaunch.js

      • .wasm + .data (:10), then meta.json as a POST_BUILD step (:64-77). Link flags:
      Flag Why
      -sENVIRONMENT=web,worker page plus the pthread workers
      -pthread -sPROXY_TO_PTHREAD main() runs on a worker; Core's file-streaming thread and the audio threads are workers too
      -sPTHREAD_POOL_SIZE=2, later 4 (:86) main + streaming thread; +2 for OpenAL Soft's mixer and event threads (a thread created with the pool empty starts only after the main thread has spun up a worker, which the audio ring would absorb as an underrun)
      -sASYNCIFY -sASYNCIFY_STACK_SIZE=262144 the blocking main loop yields once per frame; appSleep's emscripten_sleep is the other suspension. ASYNCIFY_IMPORTS is not set: the default list carries env.invoke_*, which JS exceptions under Asyncify need
      -sSTACK_SIZE=2097152 engine-ut2004's value (Emscripten's default is 64 KB)
      -sINITIAL_MEMORY=268435456 -sALLOW_MEMORY_GROWTH=1 -sMAXIMUM_MEMORY=2147483648 256 MB start, explicit 2 GB ceiling (CLAUDE.md: never unbounded)
      -sOFFSCREENCANVAS_SUPPORT "-sOFFSCREENCANVASES_TO_PTHREAD=#canvas" the canvas belongs to the engine worker so the GL device creates its context there
      -sFORCE_FILESYSTEM -sWASMFS + --js-library vendor/glue/wasmfs-shim.js + exported _wasmfs_create_jsimpl_backend, _wasmfs_create_file, _ufront_wasmfs_register, _ufront_wasmfs_set_pending files are backend files the host creates and serves (JSImpl backend); the engine opens them with ordinary POSIX calls
      -sDEFAULT_LIBRARY_FUNCS_TO_INCLUDE=['$ueWorkerBoot'] the worker-side bootstrap hangs off a __postset that is only emitted if referenced
      exported _main _malloc _free _close _ue_exec_command, runtime methods callMain stringToUTF8 UTF8ToString HEAPU8 stackSave stackAlloc stackRestore lengthBytesUTF8 PThread FS what the host calls
      --preload-file Engine/Config/web/{Default,DefUser}.ini@/System/ boot configuration from the tracked web set, never from the asset tree; appInit derives Avaris.ini/User.ini from them in the browser
      dev: -O2 -g2 -sASSERTIONS=2 -sSTACK_OVERFLOW_CHECK=2; perf: -O3 -g0 --emit-symbol-map -sASSERTIONS=0 -sSTACK_OVERFLOW_CHECK=0 the dev link at plain -g was 45 MiB and did not fit 3 GB; -O2 -g2 is 9 MiB, about 0.5 GB
      with UC2GL4ESDrv: -sGL_ENABLE_GET_PROC_ADDRESS=1 -sMIN_WEBGL_VERSION=1 -sMAX_WEBGL_VERSION=1 -sFULL_ES2=1 -sOFFSCREEN_FRAMEBUFFER gl4es targets GLES 2 = WebGL 1; emscripten_webgl_commit_frame() is a stub without -sOFFSCREEN_FRAMEBUFFER
      with WebRTCDrv: --js-library vendor/glue/webrtc-shim.js the webrtc_* imports forward to Module.UE.net

      Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 10, feature 8.

    • Source/SDLLaunch/Src/SDLLaunchWeb.cpp/.h (new, compiled only under Emscripten): UC2WebYieldFrame() = an EM_ASYNC_JS that awaits setTimeout(0) (:36-43); ue_exec_command (:50-64), the frozen ABI export (vendor/glue/ue_abi.h): runs a console command and returns its captured output (a static FString valid until the next call; "", never NULL, before the engine exists), and since a860ac8 passes what the engine does not take to the first viewport as a typed console line; UC2WebSetEngine.

    • Source/SDLLaunch/cmake/emit-artifact-meta.mjs: engine-ut2004's, targetId uc2, engineFamily UE2, buildConfig wasm-integrated, abiVersion from ue_abi.h (1), loaderStyle global-module, provenance (repo engine-uc2, git sha resolved at build time), checksums of .wasm, .js and .data, descriptive buildFlags, exports.symbolContract 1.

    • Source/UCC/CMakeLists.txt:57-81: the Node variant ucc-bin.js/.wasm: -sENVIRONMENT=node -sNODERAWFS=1 -pthread -sPROXY_TO_PTHREAD -sPTHREAD_POOL_SIZE=2 -sEXIT_RUNTIME=1 -sSTACK_SIZE=2097152 -sINITIAL_MEMORY=256MB -sALLOW_MEMORY_GROWTH=1 -sMAXIMUM_MEMORY=2GB -sASSERTIONS=0 -sSTACK_OVERFLOW_CHECK=2 -g (assertions off because SOCKFS's "select() assumes fd < 64" aborts server when NODERAWFS hands out real descriptors). It is the wasm32 test vehicle: same objects as the browser build.

  • Epic's code changed:
    • Core/Src/UnUnix.cpp:2075-2095 appSleep (above) and :3015 (no CPUSPEED=).
    • Engine/Inc/UnTex.h, UnParticleSystem.h: hand-written script mirrors wrapped in #pragma pack(push,4): wasm32 aligns DOUBLE/QWORD members to 8, i386 and Epic's /Zp4 to 4. Before the fix UTexture was 208 instead of 204 (__LastUpdateTime) and FParticleTrailData 32 instead of 28 (Time).
    • Engine/Src/UnGame.cpp:338 and Core/Src/UnMisc.cpp:1967 appIsGlobalOptionToken: a leading Key=Value token is a global option, not a map (the launcher passes the player name first; the engine tried to load a package called Name=Player).
    • SDLLaunch.cpp:242-247, 582, 613: the yield and UC2WebSetEngine; ReplaceMissingDrivers (above).
  • Frontend (per the notebooks, not read): the glue ufront-glue.js injects Module.UE and the FetchFS/WasmFS backend; targets/uc2 (target.json, manifests, precache) and the package reader for versions 145-151; e2e specs uc2-wasm-boot, -audio, -saves, -keys, -net. The host posts ufrontExec messages that wasmfs-shim.js runs through _ue_exec_command on the engine thread (calling it on the main thread would execute engine code where there is no GL context).
  • Tests: tools/dev/layout-diff.py build-wasm (159 TUs, 3,244 records: 25 differ, none script-visible, each checked; remaining risk "a raw bulk Serialize of a C++-only record against a file format"); node ucc-bin.js verifyclasses and server; the frontend specs.

How the parts fit - the native side runs the whole engine on one worker, calling UC2WebYieldFrame() when its own tick limiter did not sleep; each yield unwinds the stack with Asyncify and resumes from a 0 ms timer task, which is when SDL's input events, the host's ue_exec_command messages and the compositor run. Game bytes come from the host's JS backend through WasmFS; the engine itself does nothing web-specific when it opens a file. Without the host glue the shim throws at the first file read; without ue_exec_command the host cannot flush the config (feature 9) or run commands; without the .ini preload there is no Avaris.ini to derive.

Why - the web build must run the same engine code as native. A tab is closed, never shut down; browsers need a yielding main loop; the module is threaded because the engine needs a streaming thread and OpenAL Soft needs its own.

Decisions and alternatives

  • JavaScript exceptions, not -fwasm-exceptions. Emscripten 5.0.7 fails wasm-opt with -fwasm-exceptions -sASYNCIFY (Module::getFunction: __asyncify_get_call_index does not exist); JS exceptions with Asyncify work (measured with a throw, a catch and an emscripten_sleep inside the try). UC2's loaders throw on a missing package/object and catch a few frames up; 56 of 58 maps import the unbuilt Editor.TransBuffer, so that throw is ordinary control flow and Emscripten's default (catch blocks compiled away, first throw aborts) cannot be used.
  • engine-ut2004's "Asyncify fail-soft loader patches" were not ported (they compile catching off and patch every loader to return soft); real catching was used instead. Confirmed in the code: no FreshLinker or fail-soft branch exists in Core or Engine. (Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 10, feature 8.)
  • Keep the file-streaming thread on the web (idles at 1 ms; nothing creates streams yet).
  • Timer task (setTimeout(0)) rather than a MessagePort task for the frame yield: engine-ut2004 measured (ufront 2.43) that a timer task also gives the compositor a rendering opportunity between frames.
  • 2 GB maximum, not the toolchain default and not the 4 GB wasm32 ceiling: the owner's rule, with room to raise it "deliberately, with a measurement" (Web.cmake comment). The frontend keeps multi-GB asset residency in JS typed arrays, outside linear memory.
  • Dev builds drop DWARF at link (-g2); debug the web engine as wasm-debug under Node, not in a browser.

Disadvantages, risks, deviations

  • Cost of JS exceptions with Asyncify (an invoke_* trampoline around every call that can throw, Asyncify instrumentation around those) is called "an M11 performance question" in the notebook; no measurement of it is recorded.
  • Threaded-only: the page needs cross-origin isolation (SharedArrayBuffer) and OffscreenCanvas; meta.json says so. A browser or context without them cannot run the build.
  • meta.json is less informative than the template intends: buildFlags is hand-maintained and omits the pool size and the GL4ES flags; toolchain lists only the Emscripten version although gl4es and OpenAL Soft are fetched for the web build (Web.cmake passes no --dep-*); the file's header comment still says "no fetched libraries on the web yet". (Superseded (2026-10-09/10): see section 60, 10, feature 8.)
  • Pool size is a hard-coded count (4) tied to what OpenAL Soft's web backend starts; a new thread user needs the number raised.
  • Node cannot be used to test networking: its socket emulation has no SO_BROADCAST, so ucc-bin.js server loads the map and then stops with Dedicated server can't listen (accepted for W0).
  • Memory: the browser's PSS peak was 1.5-1.8 GB in the specs (the whole browser, not only the module's linear memory, which starts at 256 MB and may grow to the 2 GB maximum). The notebooks give no figure for the linear memory the largest map needs, so the headroom under 2 GB is not established.
  • ue_exec_command shares one static result string: not re-entrant.

Status - measured / recorded: W0 node ucc-bin.js verifyclasses -> All native class layouts match; server DM-Deadbolt -> level up for play (then the expected listen failure). W1 artifact (before the -O2 -g2 dev link of 429c17b): SDLLaunch.js 782 KB, .wasm 47 MB (dev, DWARF), .data 18 KB; after it the dev module is about 9 MiB. W2, the first browser boot, through the frontend's own harness under bin/capped and the memory guard: 1 passed (11.1s), crossOriginIsolated: true, menu level up, browser peak 1.5 GB, run peak 3.2 GB, machine peak 9.4 GB (LOG.md 2026-10-04). Later coordinator runs: uc2-wasm-boot 3/3 then 4/4, uc2-wasm-audio 3/3, saves 1/1, keys 1/1, net 2/2 (LOG.md 2026-10-06..08). Published to the preview 2026-10-07 09:35. Not verified here: frame rate and load time in a real browser on a user's machine; browsers other than the headless Chromium of the frontend's specs.

Off switch - none: the web build is a different artifact. -sMAXIMUM_MEMORY is a link flag in two files.

#9. -ini: launch overrides, FLUSHCONFIG and the config-cache flush fix (C-1, runtime part of C-2)

What the player/owner sees - the web launcher (and any command line) can set an ini value for a launch: -ini:User:[DefaultPlayer]:Name=Anubis, -ini:System:[Engine.Engine]:Language=int. The player name the launcher sends becomes the engine's default name. Settings the game only sets in memory are no longer lost.

Where (commits 7bfdd62 Core: port engine-ut2004's -ini:<File>:[<Section>]:<Key>=<Value> command-line overrides (appApplyIniOverrides), f14ba00 Core: write the dirty config files before the cache is dropped (RemoveNames); tools: c1-ini-overrides.sh, 61c9f48 Engine: FLUSHCONFIG console command ... c1-saves.sh, 604a8a3 for the global-option token)

  • Epic's code changed:
    • Core/Src/UnMisc.cpp:2002 appApplyIniOverrides (about 60 lines with its comment) and its call in appInit right after GConfig->Init (:2156), before the language lookup, so even Engine.Engine Language can be overridden; declaration Core/Inc/UnFile.h:314. Each -ini: token is split into file, section, key, value; the file part uses the config cache's generic names (System -> Avaris.ini = GIni, User -> User.ini) or a literal ini name; the value is stored with SetString (the ini is marked dirty). A token that starts with -ini: and cannot be parsed is logged (Warning: Ini override ignored (expected -ini:<File>:[<Section>]:<Key>=<Value>): <token>) and skipped, as are empty file/section/key; engine-ut2004 skips silently. A value with spaces must reach the process as one token in double quotes (the launchers join argv with plain spaces).
    • Core/Src/UnMisc.cpp:1967 appIsGlobalOptionToken (Key=Value with a non-empty key and no ? before the =), used by UnGame.cpp:338 so a leading -ini:... or Name=x token still boots the default map.
    • Core/Src/UnObj.cpp:5459-5495 (UObject::RemoveNames, called by every PurgeGarbage): GConfig->Flush(0) (:5476) before GConfig->UnloadAll() (:5493), #ifndef _XBOX. Epic's own Flush at the top of the function (:5463) was #if 0.
    • Engine/Src/UnEngine.cpp:308-322: FLUSHCONFIG console command first in UEngine::Exec: GConfig->Flush(0) and the reply ufront: flushed config to disk (ok).
  • script: none. Frontend: ufrontend/packages/glue/src/config/ini-identity.js iniOverrideArg emits the tokens; ufrontend/packages/frontend/src/shell/boot.ts:752 calls ueExecResult('flushconfig', 4000) through ue_exec_command before it reads the inis (per web-ini-saves.md; not re-read). Frontend commit d6554d4 (LOG.md).
  • Tests: tools/dev/c1-ini-overrides.sh (30 checks, 0 failures at logs/c1/c1-ini-run4.txt): a system key and a user key reach the class defaults (proven by the console command get <Class> <Property>, not by the engine's own log line), a quoted token with spaces, ? and = arrives whole, an -ini: token as the first token still boots to the menu, seven malformed tokens are logged and ignored without stopping the good token after them, ucc-bin applies them too, the values are in the ini after a SIGTERM exit, FLUSHCONFIG writes them to a SIGKILLed client. tools/dev/c1-saves.sh and c1-web-saves.sh (feature 10).

How the parts fit - the native side reads the switch once in appInit; both launchers (SDLLaunch, UCC) go through appInit, so one call covers both. Without the frontend calling flushconfig the web persistence daemon would not see values that were only set; without the RemoveNames fix the values would be dropped at the next garbage purge on every platform.

Why

  • The launcher's "Player Name" box emits -ini:User:[DefaultPlayer]:Name=<name>; nothing read it until the switch was ported.
